# Skills · K · part 23

800 entries, klartext to klussenbeheer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **klartext**: Rewrites, audits or drafts text so it sounds like a real person instead of an AI, removing stock phrases, staged reveals and filler while keeping the writer's meaning and voice. Works in German and English. Use when the user says "Klartext", "plain talk", "humanize", "klingt nach KI", "sounds like AI", "entschwurble", "remove AI slop", or asks to make store texts, posts, READMEs, applications or emails sound natural. ([ui-edi/skills](https://github.com/ui-edi/skills/tree/HEAD/klartext))
- **klarun**: Use when the user asks about their company's data team in Klarun, such as reports and dashboards, data assets and lineage, team members, OKRs, business domains, FinOps budget, actuals and cloud cost, Microsoft Copilot adoption or their Klarun notifications, or asks to add or update any of these in Klarun. ([klarun-app/klarun-plugins](https://github.com/klarun-app/klarun-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/klarun/skills/klarun))
- **klasika-expert**: Domain-expert agent for vážná hudba (klasika + soudobá vážná + jazz s klasickým přesahem). Pulls Petr's Spotify classical/jazz taste + Discogs collection + hand-edited preferences, scrapes upcoming concerts of the favourite Prague orchestras via static scrapers, hits a list of festival URLs via WebFetch, and produces a ranked top-N list of candidates as structured JSON. **Sends no email — output is consumed by the kulturni-prehled aggregator.** Can also be invoked directly when the user wants a klasika snapshot in chat. ([petronijus/kulturniprehled](https://github.com/petronijus/kulturniprehled/tree/HEAD/skills/klasika-expert))
- **klasr-product**: Klasr domain knowledge — product vision, personas, data flows, eco-design rules, REAC/jury constraints. Use when planning, reviewing, or making any product or architecture decision. ([ColJurten/klasr-loop](https://github.com/ColJurten/klasr-loop/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/klasr-product))
- **klassci-audit-comptable**: Audit E2E du module comptable KLASSCI (multi-tenant) — UI + code + CLI. À utiliser pour "audit compta", "vérifier les KPIs", "rapprocher dashboard et CLI", "trouver des bugs sur les pages comptables", "audit OHADA". Enrichit automatiquement docs/audits/&lt;date&gt;-audit-comptable-klassci.md et propose des rules nouvelles si nécessaire. ([James10192/KLASSCIv2](https://github.com/James10192/KLASSCIv2/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/klassci-audit-comptable) · ★1)

- **klassci-test-bts-lmd-matrix**: Exécute la matrice complète de tests BTS/LMD (4 combos × Unit + Feature + Browser) avec rapport tableau croisé pass/fail par combo et par couche ([James10192/KLASSCIv2](https://github.com/James10192/KLASSCIv2/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/klassci-test-bts-lmd-matrix) · ★1)
- **klassci-test-e2e**: Rejouer un test end-to-end KLASSCI supérieur sur un tenant réel. PRIORITÉ ABSOLUE = valider le fix ou la feature livré(e) dans la session courante (le code qu'on vient de merger/déployer), via logs avant/après + route affectée authentifiée + écran navigateur. Le flux BTS tronc commun n'est qu'UN scénario de régression parmi d'autres, pas la cible par défaut. Diagnostics klassci-cli, mutations API CLI, seed académique contrôlée. À utiliser pour "continue le test e2e", "valide de bout en bout", "teste sur presentation", "valide mon fix/feature", "BTS TC end-to-end". ([James10192/KLASSCIv2](https://github.com/James10192/KLASSCIv2/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/klassci-test-e2e) · ★1)
- **klassci-user-tutorial**: Génère un tutoriel utilisateur illustré pour une fonctionnalité KLASSCI (personnel d'école : secrétariat, coordination, comptabilité, enseignants), avec de VRAIES captures d'écran annotées et un PDF soigné. Utiliser quand on demande un "tuto", "guide utilisateur", "mode d'emploi", "pas à pas", "expliquer à une directrice/secrétaire comment faire", ou de répondre à un utilisateur qui ne sait pas où cliquer dans KLASSCI. ([James10192/KLASSCIv2](https://github.com/James10192/KLASSCIv2/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/klassci-user-tutorial) · ★1)

- **klaus-code**: INVOKE WHEN: User runs /klaus-code with a task README path or folder (usually under ~/memory/), or asks to do the task described in such a README. COVERS: Reading the task README and its linked ticket/spec, branching, implementing, running tests and lint, a self-review loop with /review, grouping commits with /atomic-commits, confirming commits with the user, opening a draft PR, and filling it with /describe-pr. DO NOT INVOKE FOR: Writing the task README itself, tasks started from a Linear issue (implement-linear-issue), or one-off questions with no code change. ([kaustubhhiware/dotfiles](https://github.com/kaustubhhiware/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/klaus-code))
- **klaus-find**: Use before creating any new UI component file, and before adding a component to a screen that has no component for the job yet.
